Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，AdaTok-Full使用256个token达到rFID 1.31；AdaTok-Adaptive平均仅使用约118个token即达到rFID 1.50，优于相同预算下的离散1D基线方法。在自回归图像生成中，自适应短表示相比固定256-token解码获得约2.1倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 视觉token数量可以作为内容条件化的输出被学习，而非设定为固定超参数。表示与分配的协同设计是实现可操作弹性分词的关键，AdaTok证明了离散视觉分词器能够在单次前向传播中自主完成预算分配，同时兼顾重建质量与计算效率。

Abstract: Image tokenizers, from 2D grids to recent 1D sequences, typically encode every image with the same fixed number of tokens. Yet visual complexity is highly heterogeneous, so a uniform budget overspends on simple inputs and underserves complex ones. Existing elastic tokenizers expose variable-length reconstructions, but often leave token length as a deployment-time operating point, a search target, or an external prediction rather than an output of the tokenizer itself. In this work, we ask whether a discrete visual tokenizer can budget itself in one pass. Our central finding is that actionable elasticity requires a representation--allocation co-design: prefixes must remain decodable across budgets, and the tokenizer must learn which prefix each image needs. We propose AdaTok, a self-budgeting discrete 1D tokenizer. AdaTok combines Prioritized Representation Learning, which orders tokens with nested tail masking and resolves budget-dependent semantic shift through Multi-Head LoRA decoder heads, with Adaptive Token Allocation, which trains a lightweight deterministic-group GRPO policy over candidate budgets. Dynamic Pareto Weighting balances fidelity and efficiency during policy training without manual trade-off sweeps. On ImageNet-1K, AdaTok-Full reaches rFID 1.31 at 256 tokens, while AdaTok-Adaptive attains rFID 1.50 using only ~118 tokens on average, outperforming discrete 1D baselines at comparable budgets. In autoregressive image generation, the shorter adaptive representation yields ~2.1x throughput over a fixed 256-token decode, suggesting that visual token count can be learned as a content-conditioned output rather than set as a fixed hyperparameter.

</details>


### [69] [DualGate-Net: A Prior-Gated Dual-Encoder Framework for Histopathology Cell Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07222)
*Bahman Jafari Tabaghsar,Son Tran,K. Devaraja,Atul Sajjanhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DualGate-Net，一种先验感知的双编码器框架，通过可学习的先验门控融合机制自适应整合组织上下文信息，用于组织病理学图像中的细胞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理学图像中的细胞检测高度依赖周围组织上下文，视觉上相似的细胞在不同微环境下可能属于不同类别。现有的组织感知方法虽然引入了上下文先验，但通常依赖静态融合策略，可能传播噪声信息，导致检测性能受限。因此需要一种自适应调节组织先验影响力的方法。

Method: 提出DualGate-Net框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 双编码器结构——基于ConvNeXtV2的局部编码器提取细胞级特征，基于SegFormer的全局编码器捕获组织级上下文；(2) 可学习的先验门控融合机制（Prior-Gated Fusion），在不同空间位置自适应调控组织先验的影响权重，避免噪声传播；(3) 辅助前景重建分支，在训练中保留高频细胞结构信息；此外还引入辅助的cellness引导线索以增强定位鲁棒性。

Result: 在OCELOT基准数据集上，DualGate-Net取得了验证集macro F1为0.7722、测试集macro F1为0.7345的成绩，相比现有方法实现了一致性的性能提升，验证了自适应先验融合策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应先验整合对于鲁棒的组织病理学细胞检测至关重要。DualGate-Net通过可学习的门控机制动态调节组织先验的贡献，结合前景重建和cellness引导等辅助策略，有效提升了细胞检测性能，证明了在细胞检测中智能融合局部与全局信息优于静态融合策略。

Abstract: Cell detection in histopathology images strongly depends on surrounding tissue context, where visually similar cells may belong to different classes under different microenvironments. Recent tissue-aware methods incorporate contextual priors, but often rely on static fusion strategies that may propagate noisy information. In this work, we propose DualGate-Net, a prior-aware dual-encoder framework that combines a ConvNeXtV2-based local encoder and a SegFormer-based global encoder through a learnable prior-gated fusion mechanism. The proposed module adaptively regulates the influence of tissue priors across spatial locations, while an auxiliary foreground reconstruction branch preserves high-frequency cellular structures during training. In addition, auxiliary cellness-guided cues are incorporated to further improve localization robustness. Experiments on the OCELOT benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements, achieving macro F1-scores of 0.7722 on the validation set and 0.7345 on the test set, highlighting the effectiveness of adaptive prior integration for robust histopathology cell detection.

</details>


### [70] [Does Appearance Help? A Systematic Study of Image-Based Re-Identification in Online 3D Multi-Pedestrian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07233)
*Eduardo Borges,Luís Garrote,Urbano J. Nunes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于图像的ReID在在线3D多目标跟踪中的应用，提出了一种轻量级投影框架，将几何建模与外观建模解耦，并通过级联匹配策略有效恢复遮挡目标，在保持低延迟的同时增强移动机器人的跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LiDAR的3D多目标跟踪仅依赖几何信息，在长时间遮挡或拥挤人群环境中难以有效区分目标身份。虽然融合基于RGB的重识别（ReID）理论上可以保留身份上下文，但现有方法通常依赖计算开销大的并行检测器，无法满足移动机器人的实时响应需求。因此需要一种轻量级方案，在计算延迟与跟踪鲁棒性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的基于投影的框架，将几何建模与外观建模解耦。系统地分析了多种特征提取架构（包括轻量级CNN和Vision Transformer），并评估了多种多模态数据关联策略，包括朴素线性融合和级联匹配策略，以平衡计算延迟与跟踪鲁棒性。

Result: 在KITTI数据集行人类别上的实验表明：（1）外观代价与运动代价的朴素线性融合会因视觉噪声导致性能下降；（2）级联匹配策略能够在不损害整体精度的前提下成功恢复被遮挡的轨迹，有效防止身份切换，维持人机交互的连续性；（3）轻量级架构能够在安全导航所需的低延迟与社交感知所需的判别能力之间实现最优平衡。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构能够在移动机器人安全导航所需的低延迟和社交感知所需的判别力之间提供最佳折衷。级联匹配策略优于简单线性融合，是将外观信息有效融入3D MOT的更优数据关联方案，能够在拥挤和遮挡场景中维持稳定的身份跟踪。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) typically relies solely on geometric information, which is often insufficient to distinguish between targets during prolonged occlusions or in crowded human-populated environments. While integrating RGB-based Re-Identification (ReID) offers a theoretical solution for preserving identity context, existing approaches often rely on computationally expensive parallel detectors that hinder real-time robot responsiveness. This work presents a systematic study of image-based ReID in online 3D MOT, utilizing a lightweight projection-based framework to decouple geometric and appearance modeling for mobile robots. A comprehensive analysis of feature extraction architectures is conducted, employing lightweight CNNs and Vision Transformers, and evaluating various multi-modal data association strategies to balance computational latency with robust tracking. Experiments on the Pedestrian class of the KITTI dataset reveal that naive linear fusion, of appearance and motion costs, degrades performance due to visual noise. Conversely, a cascaded matching strategy successfully recovers occluded tracks without compromising overall precision, effectively preventing identity switches to maintain human-robot interaction continuity. We show that lightweight architectures can offer an optimal trade-off between the low latency required for safe navigation and the discriminative power needed for social awareness.

</details>


### [71] [Reconstructing Multi-Decadal Forest Disturbances: A Spatio-Temporal Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07249)
*Linus Scheibenreif,Anton Raichuk,Maxim Neumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Vision Transformer的深度学习框架，同时建模时间轨迹和空间邻域信息，实现美国本土38年（1984-2022）森林扰动的时空连贯制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确监测森林扰动对理解碳动态和土地管理至关重要，但传统方法通常依赖逐像素的卫星时间序列分析，忽略了空间上下文信息，导致结果存在空间噪声和伪影。本文旨在通过引入时空联合建模来克服这一局限。

Method: 采用Vision Transformer架构构建时空深度学习框架，同时对时间轨迹和空间邻域进行建模。利用多源卫星数据（Landsat、Sentinel-1、Sentinel-2），在两种时间窗口（38年和近6年）上进行训练。该方法能有效过滤弱监督信号中的噪声，生成空间连贯的森林扰动图。验证基于一个新构建的人工标注数据集（n=300）和独立的火灾边界数据集（MTBS, n=706）进行全面评估。

Result: 模型展现出较高精度：在MTBS火灾数据集上±1年检测精确率高达98.2%，F1值最高75.8%；在CONUS验证数据集上精确率达71.3%，F1值达47.3%。时空模型有效减少了空间伪影，但在不同扰动类型之间存在性能权衡，相较于逐像素基线方法在某些扰动模式下表现各有优劣。

Conclusion: 时空联合建模能有效提升森林扰动制图的空间连贯性并降低噪声，但该任务本身极具挑战性，不同扰动类型之间存在性能折衷。该方法为一致性森林监测提供了有前景的基础框架。

Abstract: Accurate monitoring of forest disturbances is essential for understanding carbon dynamics and land management, yet traditional approaches typically rely on pixel-wise analysis of satellite time-series, ignoring spatial context. We present a deep learning framework that maps 38 years (1984-2022) of forest disturbance across the contiguous United States by modeling temporal trajectories and spatial neighborhoods simultaneously. By leveraging a vision transformer architecture, our approach effectively filters noise from weak supervision signals to produce spatially coherent disturbance maps. We perform exhaustive evaluations across multiple satellites (Landsat, Sentinel-1, Sentinel-2) and temporal windows (38 years and the more recent 6 years), validating performance against a novel, manually annotated validation dataset (n=300) and independent fire perimeter dataset (n=706). The results highlight the complexity of the task: while our spatio-temporal model demonstrates high precision (up to 98.2% for +-1 year detection on MTBS and up to 71.3% on the CONUS validation datasets, with F1-scores up to 75.8% and 47.3%, respectively) and effectively reduces spatial artifacts, it exhibits performance trade-offs across different disturbance regimes compared to pixel-wise baselines. Our method offers a promising foundation for consistent forest monitoring.

</details>


### [72] [Geometric-Aware Hypergraph Reasoning for Novel Class Discovery in Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07280)
*Zihao Zhang,Aming Wu,Yang Li,Yahong Han,Jialie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于超图的框架用于点云分割中的新类发现，通过建模类间高阶关联和几何感知原型，实现从已知类到新类的协同推理，提升分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点云分割中新类发现方法主要依赖成对关联进行类别分配和新类推理，难以捕捉已知类与新类之间的复杂高阶关系，可能导致语义分割不准确；同时，现有方法过度关注语义特征提取，对点云中几何信息的利用不足，限制了对空间结构的理解。

Method: 提出两个核心设计：（1）基于超图的框架，通过超边建模类间高阶关联，突破传统成对关系的局限，实现从已知类到新类的协同推理；（2）几何感知原型（Geometric-Aware Prototypes），增强类级别几何线索的表征能力，并通过超边传播几何信息，改善对跨类空间分布的理解。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SemanticPOSS两个数据集上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性和优越性，证明了超图高阶关联建模和几何感知原型对新类发现点云分割任务的提升作用。

Conclusion: 超图建模的高阶类间关联和几何感知原型能有效弥补现有方法在复杂关系捕捉和几何信息利用方面的不足，显著提升点云分割中新类发现的性能。

Abstract: Novel class discovery in point cloud segmentation aims to transfer knowledge from known classes to automatically identify and segment unlabeled novel classes in point clouds. Existing methods mainly rely on pairwise associations for class assignment and novel class reasoning, which limits their ability to capture complex relationships among known and novel classes and may lead to inaccurate semantic segmentation. To address this issue, we introduce a hypergraph-based framework that models high-order associations among classes and enables collaborative reasoning from known classes to novel classes beyond traditional pairwise relations. Moreover, existing methods tend to focus on semantic feature extraction while paying insufficient attention to geometric information in point clouds. To better exploit spatial structure, we propose Geometric-Aware Prototypes to enhance the representation of class-level geometric cues. By propagating geometric information through hyperedges, the proposed method improves the understanding of spatial distributions across classes and leads to more accurate segmentation. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI and SemanticPOSS datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method.

</details>


### [73] [ExMesh: EXplicit Mesh Reconstruction with Topology Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07288)
*Chuanjin Fan,Lifan Wu,Wenjie Chang,Hanzhi Chang,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExMesh提出了一种直接优化显式网格的新框架，通过将可微优化与离散拓扑更新相结合，实现从多视图图像重建高质量表面网格，无需中间表示或后处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图表面重建方法依赖中间表示（如隐式场）和后处理步骤（如Marching Cubes或TSDF融合），常导致伪影和碎片化几何。直接优化显式网格虽然前景广阔，但面临两个关键挑战：（1）如何自适应地细化网格拓扑以捕捉细节且不引入退化面；（2）在网格结构演化过程中如何维护一致的UV坐标以支持高保真纹理。

Method: 提出ExMesh框架，将可微优化与离散拓扑更新无缝集成。核心技术包括：（1）自适应顶点分裂与合并策略，实现从粗到精的优化同时保持几何完整性；（2）实时UV坐标维护机制，确保网格拓扑变化时纹理映射的一致性。据作者所知，ExMesh是首个将离散拓扑操作无缝集成到连续可微优化流程中的框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，ExMesh在精度、计算效率和网格简洁性之间实现了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: ExMesh通过将离散拓扑操作与连续可微优化相结合，成功解决了直接显式网格优化中的拓扑自适应细化和UV一致性维护两大难题，为多视图表面重建提供了一种无需中间表示和后处理的端到端解决方案，在精度、效率和网格质量方面取得了综合优势。

Abstract: Reconstructing surface meshes from multi-view images has remained a core challenge in recent years. Most existing methods, whether implicit or explicit, depend on intermediate representations and post-processing steps like Marching Cubes or TSDF fusion, often resulting in artifacts and fragmented geometry. Directly optimizing explicit meshes is a promising approach. However, it presents two critical challenges. The first is how to adaptively refine mesh topology to capture detail without introducing degenerate faces. The second is how to maintain consistent UV coordinates for high-fidelity texturing as the mesh structure evolves. To overcome these, we propose ExMesh, a novel framework that directly optimizes explicit meshes by integrating differentiable optimization with discrete topology updates. Specifically, we introduce an adaptive vertex splitting and merging strategy, along with real-time UV maintenance, to enable coarse-to-fine optimization while preserving geometric integrity. To our knowledge, ExMesh is the first framework to seamlessly integrate discrete topology operations into a continuous differentiable optimization pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ExMesh achieves a balance among accuracy, computational efficiency, and mesh conciseness.

</details>


### [74] [CULTURESCORE: Evaluating Cultural Faithfulness in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07311)
*Anku Rani,Wei Dai,Shravan Nayak,Pattie Maes,Mahdi M. Kalayeh,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CultureScore框架，从身份、语境和行为三个维度评估视频生成模型的文化忠实度，发现当前最先进模型的文化忠实度仅达56.8%，且视觉质量高的模型反而文化表现最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Veo 3.1和LTX-2等视频生成模型的进步，其准确表征全球多元文化的能力仍是一个关键但研究不足的前沿问题。现有评估指标（如VideoScore）仅衡量视觉质量，无法评估文化忠实度——例如将Namaste替换为握手的模型与正确生成手势的模型得分相同，这暴露了评估体系的重大缺陷。

Method: 提出CultureScore组合评估框架，将文化忠实度分解为三个细粒度维度：身份（Identity，谁被表征）、语境（Context，文化本地化的背景）和行为（Behavior，规范性手势与交互）。通过覆盖10个国家的评估套件进行实验，使用三个最先进模型生成6,180个视频进行系统评估。

Result: 没有现有模型能实现文化忠实的视频生成：表现最佳的模型整体CultureScore仅为56.8%；行为维度最具挑战性，所有模型均低于52%。人类偏好排名与CultureScore方向一致，但与VideoScore呈反转关系——视觉质量得分最高的模型被标注者排名最末。

Conclusion: 文化忠实度是公平视频生成的关键标准，仅依赖视觉质量指标不足以衡量模型的真实表现。CultureScore框架揭示了当前视频生成模型在文化表征方面的系统性不足，强调了开发文化感知评估与生成方法的迫切需求。

Abstract: As video generation models like Veo 3.1 and LTX-2 advance, their ability to accurately represent diverse global cultures remains a critical yet understudied frontier. Current metrics, such as VideoScore, only measure visual quality but offer no mechanism for assessing cultural faithfulness. Consequently, a model that replaces a Namaste with a handshake receives the same score as one that generates the gesture correctly. We propose CultureScore, a compositional evaluation framework that decomposes cultural faithfulness into three granular dimensions: Identity (who is represented), Context (culturally localized background), and Behavior (normative gestures and interactions). We operationalize this framework through an evaluation suite spanning 10 countries, yielding 6,180 generated videos across three state-of-the-art models. Our evaluation reveals that no current model achieves culturally faithful video generation: the best-performing model reaches only 56.8\% overall CultureScore, with Behavior the most challenging dimension, which remains below 52\% across all models. Furthermore, human preference rankings align directionally with CultureScore but are inverted relative to VideoScore; the highest-scoring model on visual quality was ranked last by annotators, underscoring that cultural faithfulness is an essential criterion for equitable video generation.

</details>


### [75] [AnchorWorld: Embodied Egocentric World Simulation with View-based Evolution Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07326)
*Yu Li,Menghan Xia,Gongye Liu,Xintao Wang,Conglang Zhang,Lei Ke,Yuxuan Lin,Ruihang Chu,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorWorld提出了一种以3D人体运动为交互模态的自我中心世界模拟框架，通过辅助外部视角训练监督增强交互完整性，并利用锚定视图与文本描述实现灵活的世界自定义演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界建模作为关键前沿方向，在实际应用所需的多功能可控性方面仍未被充分探索。现有方法难以处理自我中心视角中人体部位被遮挡或截断的问题，同时缺乏灵活的世界自定义和动态演化机制。本文旨在弥补这一空白，提升交互完整性和世界可定制性。

Method: AnchorWorld框架包含两大核心设计：(1) 以3D人体运动作为主要交互模态，引入辅助训练监督——利用与智能体第一人称感知解耦的外部视角（exogenous viewpoints），使模型能观察到智能体相对于环境的完整身体姿态，从而增强人-世界交互的空间定位鲁棒性；(2) 提出一种简洁有效的世界自定义演化机制，通过在统一世界坐标系中定义锚定视图（anchor views），结合文本描述来指定局部场景的动态演化。

Result: 实验结果表明AnchorWorld显著优于当前最先进的基线方法；消融实验验证了各关键设计的有效性；世界自定义方案展现出良好的时空几何一致性，并严格遵循预设的动态演化规则。

Conclusion: AnchorWorld通过外部视角辅助监督和锚定视图自定义机制，有效提升了自我中心世界模拟中的交互完整性和可控性，在性能上显著超越现有方法，为交互式世界建模的实用化提供了新的解决路径。

Abstract: Despite being a pivotal frontier, interactive world modeling remains underexplored in terms of the versatile controllability required by practical scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present AnchorWorld, a framework that advances egocentric simulation through enhanced interaction integrity and a flexible mechanism for world customization. First, we utilize 3D human motion as the primary interaction modality. To complement the out-of-view or truncated body parts in egocentric views, we introduce an auxiliary training supervision that incorporates exogenous viewpoints decoupled from the agent's first-person sensorium. It allows the model to observe the agent's full-body positioning relative to the environment, facilitating a more robust spatial grounding of human-world interactions. Furthermore, we propose a simple yet effective mechanism for customizing self-evolving worlds. This is achieved by defining anchor views within a unified world coordinate system, coupled with textual descriptions dictating the dynamic evolution of local scenes. Experimental results show that AnchorWorld significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, while ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our key designs. Notably, our customization scheme exhibits promising spatio-temporal geometric consistency and adheres strictly to the prescribed evolutionary dynamics.

</details>


### [76] [Varifold Moment Invariants for Sustainable and Explainable Contour Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07333)
*G. Longari,J. -C. Alvarez Paiva,A. B. Tumpach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI)作为统一框架，整合多种已有的矩不变量方法，结合轻量分类器在多个形状分类数据集上以低计算成本取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的矩不变量方法（如扩展高斯图像、椭圆傅里叶描述符、形状分布等）各自独立发展，缺乏统一的理论框架。同时，基于轮廓的现有方法在判别能力和计算效率之间难以兼顾，尤其无法在轻量设备上高效运行。作者希望提出一种既具有强判别力、又具有清晰几何含义且计算高效的统一特征提取框架。

Method: 提出Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI)框架，利用varifold理论将区域几何、边界和切线族三者统一结合，构建平移和旋转不变的特征。该方法能够生成大量具有明确几何意义的不变特征，并将VMI特征提取与轻量级分类器（随机森林或多层感知机）相结合，实现高效分类。

Result: 在多个广泛使用的数据集（树叶、物体、细胞等不同类型）上的分类任务中，VMI方法在使用少量可几何解释的特征情况下取得了高准确率，超越了基于轮廓的现有最先进方法，同时大幅降低了计算成本，使算法可在轻量级设备上运行。

Conclusion: VMI为众多已有矩不变量方法提供了统一理论框架，通过融合区域、边界和切线的几何信息，生成具有高判别力和几何可解释性的不变特征。结合轻量分类器即可在多种形状分类任务中达到优异性能，同时显著降低计算开销，具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce Varifold Moments Invariants (VMI) as a unifying framework for many previously introduced Moment Invariants. These invariants are deeply related to other contour features that are invariant under translations and rotations, like Extended Gaussian Image, Elliptic Fourier Descriptors or Shape Distributions. The advantage of the varifold approach to moments consists in being able to combine the geometry of the region, its boundary, and the family of lines tangent to it, in order to create a substantial number of invariant features with high discriminating power and clear geometric meaning. By coupling our VMI feature extraction with the light feature classifiers Random Forest or Multi-Layer-Perceptron, we outperform state-of-the-art approaches based on contours, while decreasing drastically the computational cost to the point of allowing our algorithm to run on light devices. We tested our approach on classification tasks on a large number of widely-used datasets of various types (leaves, objects, cells) and achieved high accuracy with a low number of geometrically interpretable features.

</details>


### [77] [VeriDrive: Verifiable Counterfactual Supervision for Cost-Efficient Vision-Language Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07338)
*Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. H. Shum,Toby P. Breckon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeriDrive提出了一种面向规划的、可验证的反事实监督框架，通过结构化的感知-评估-修正链替代自由形式的驾驶推理，在降低标注成本的同时提升了视觉-语言驾驶模型的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言驾驶模型虽然越来越多地使用推理监督来连接感知、预测和规划，但现有的驾驶推理数据通常是自由形式的（free-form），且依赖前沿大模型生成，成本高昂。这种非结构化的推理缺乏可验证性和可审计性，难以在现实标注预算下高效扩展。

Method: VeriDrive框架包含以下核心设计：(1) 将驾驶推理转化为结构化的**感知-评估-修正(Perception-Evaluation-Revision)**链，将关键目标锚定在未来运动中，用规则可检验的证据评估替代自车轨迹，并将高风险意图修正为专家行为，最终生成规划目标；(2) 在数据扩展方面，结合本地生成与验证器引导的选择性纠正策略（validator-guided selective correction），仅对无效或困难样本升级调用更强的模型，从而控制成本；(3) 基于nuScenes数据集构建VeriDrive数据集，并在Omni-Q协议下进行训练。

Result: 在受控的开环实验中，VeriDrive在L2误差、碰撞率和交叉率（Intersection）指标上均优于OmniDrive基线，同时显著降低了日志token使用量、生成时间和实际LLM/VLM调用成本。

Conclusion: 可审计的中间结构化字段和结构化修正目标能够在现实标注预算约束下有效提升视觉-语言规划监督的质量。反事实的、可验证的监督范式相比自由形式推理更具成本效益和性能优势。

Abstract: Vision-language driving models increasingly use reasoning supervision to bridge perception, prediction, and planning, but existing driving rationales are often free-form and expensive to generate with frontier models. We present VeriDrive, a framework for constructing planning-oriented, verifiable counterfactual supervision. VeriDrive converts driving reasoning into a structured Perception-Evaluation-Revision chain that grounds key objects in future motion, evaluates alternative ego trajectories with rule-checkable evidence, revises risky intent toward expert behavior, and produces final planning targets. To scale data construction, VeriDrive combines local generation with validator-guided selective correction, escalating only invalid or difficult samples. We build the VeriDrive dataset on nuScenes and train under the Omni-Q protocol. Controlled open-loop experiments show that VeriDrive improves L2, Collision, and Intersection over OmniDrive while reducing logged token usage, generation time, and actual paid LLM/VLM cost. These results show that auditable intermediate fields and structured revision targets can improve vision-language planning supervision under realistic annotation budgets. Code, prompts, and validator scripts are coming soon and will be released after the review process.

</details>


### [78] [Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter for Micro-gesture Online Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07355)
*Xucheng Shen,Kun Li,Fei Wang,Wei Qian,Jin Jiang,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出时空解耦适配器和自适应软平衡增强方法，用于解决微手势在线识别中细粒度时空建模和长尾分布问题，在第四届EI-MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 2中以F1=0.43808获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微手势在线识别面临两大挑战：（1）微手势持续时间极短、运动幅度低、视觉线索模糊，现有参数高效适配器通常采用单分支联合建模时空信息，难以捕捉微手势的细粒度模式；（2）基准数据集存在长尾分布问题，影响模型对少数类的识别能力。

Method: 提出两个核心模块：（1）时空解耦适配器（Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter），通过轻量级深度可分离卷积将视频适配分解为独立的时间分支和空间分支，分别建模时间动态和空间细节；（2）自适应软平衡增强（Adaptive Soft Balanced Augmentation），根据类别稀有度和学习难度动态分配数据增强强度，无需手动设定阈值。

Result: 方法在第四届EI-MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 2（微手势在线识别）中取得F1分数0.43808，排名第一。

Conclusion: 时空解耦的适配策略能够更有效地捕捉微手势的判别性时空表征，结合基于类别稀有度和学习难度的自适应增强策略可有效缓解长尾分布问题，整体方案在微手势在线识别任务上取得了最优性能。

Abstract: Micro-gesture online recognition aims to temporally localize and classify subtle gestures in untrimmed videos. Owing to their extremely short duration, low motion amplitude, and ambiguous visual cues, capturing discriminative spatiotemporal representations remains highly challenging. Existing parameter-efficient adapters typically employ a single branch to model spatial and temporal cues jointly, which may fail to capture the fine-grained patterns of micro-gestures. To address this limitation, we propose a Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter that decomposes video adaptation into independent temporal and spatial branches via lightweight depthwise convolutions. In addition, to address the long-tail distribution problem in the benchmark dataset, we introduce Adaptive Soft Balanced Augmentation, which dynamically allocates augmentation intensity based on class rarity and learning difficulty, without manual thresholds. Our method achieves an F1 score of 0.43808, ranking 1st in Track 2 of the 4th EI-MiGA-IJCAI Challenge.

</details>


### [79] [Dash2Sim: Closed-Loop Driving Simulation from in-the-wild Dashcam Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07366)
*Anurag Ghosh,Francesco Pittaluga,Khiem Vuong,Angela Chen,Juan Alvarez-Padilla,Manmohan Chandraker,Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dash2Sim框架将野外单目行车记录仪视频转化为可用于仿真的度量化、地理参考的4D驾驶日志，并构建了包含4,244个施工区场景的ROADWork4D基准数据集，揭示了施工区场景对现有规划器的挑战，同时证明其恢复的稠密深度可提升新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真通常依赖少数城市采集的数据或人工编写的合成场景，覆盖范围有限，难以涵盖长尾场景（如施工区）。行车记录仪视频覆盖广泛的地理位置和驾驶情况，包括罕见场景，但从野外单目视频中恢复精确的4D场景十分困难，导致其在仿真中的可用性受限。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，将大规模野外行车记录仪视频转化为可直接用于仿真的驾驶日志，特别关注施工区这一重要的长尾场景类别。

Method: 提出Dash2Sim框架，将野外单目行车记录仪视频转化为度量化（metric）、地理参考（geo-referenced）的4D驾驶日志，兼容现有仿真器。每个生成的驾驶日志通过独立维护的地图进行无标注验证。该框架可恢复稠密深度信息，并应用于大规模视频语料库，构建了ROADWork4D基准数据集。在验证子集ROADWork4D-CL上进行闭环规划器评估实验。

Result: （1）构建了ROADWork4D基准数据集，涵盖17个城市的4,244个场景和270万个3D物体；（2）在验证子集ROADWork4D-CL（2,201个场景）上评估闭环规划器，发现施工区场景具有高难度：基于规则和混合规划器的泛化能力优于纯学习方法，但所有规划器均无法完成施工区临时车道引导所需的变道操作；（3）Dash2Sim恢复的稠密深度在感知指标上将新视角合成质量提升最高达19%。

Conclusion: Dash2Sim证明了从野外单目行车记录仪视频生成高质量仿真数据的可行性，所构建的ROADWork4D数据集填补了施工区这一长尾场景的数据空白。实验表明当前规划器在施工区场景中表现不足，凸显了该基准的研究价值。同时，Dash2Sim恢复的深度信息对闭环传感器仿真中的新视角合成具有显著增益，展示了其作为单目视频驱动仿真基础设施的广泛潜力。

Abstract: Self-driving simulations typically rely on data collected in a small number of cities or on hand-authored synthetic scenarios. Dashcam videos cover a far broader range of locations and situations, including rare or long-tailed scenarios. They are considered less usable for simulation because it is difficult to recover accurate 4D scenes from monocular in-the-wild videos. Work zones are one such class of long-tailed situations that dashcams capture. We present Dash2Sim, a framework that turns in-the-wild monocular dashcam videos into metric, geo-referenced 4D driving logs compatible with existing simulators, and verifies eachone against an independently maintained map without annotations. We apply Dash2Sim to a large video corpus to create the ROADWork4D benchmark dataset, which spans 4,244 scenes with 2.7M 3D objects across 17 cities. On a verified subset ROADWork4D-CL (2,201 scenes), we study privileged closed-loop planners and find that work zone scenarios are difficult: while rule-based and hybrid planners generalize better than learning-based ones, all fall short, failing to make the lane changes that temporary work zone channels require. Beyond planning, dense depth recovered by Dash2Sim improves novel-view synthesis quality by up to 19% on perceptual metrics, suggesting its potential to provide rich conditioning for closed-loop sensor simulation from monocular videos.

</details>


### [80] [Mitosis Detection in the Wild: Multi-Tumor and Context-Aware Generalization in the MIDOG 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07368)
*Marc Aubreville,Jonas Ammeling,Sweta Banerjee,Viktoria Weiss,Taryn A. Donovan,Robert Klopfleisch,Jiaqi Lv,Shan E Ahmed Raza,Raphaël Bourgade,Thomas Walter,Yasemin Topuz,Songül Varlı,Charles-Antoine Collins-Fekete,Zhuoyan Shen,Navya Sri Kelam,Nitin Singhal,Christian Marzahl,Brian Napora,Tengyou Xu,Hongyan Gu,Mario Vento,Gennaro Percannella,Norbert Ropiak,Izabela Wasiak,Jie Xiao,Shaojun Liu,Seungho Choe,April Khademi,Vidushi Walia,Sujatha Kotte,Andrew Broad,Alex Wright,Guillaume Balezo,Esha Sadia Nasir,Mostafa Jahanifar,Yosuke Yamagishi,Shouhei Hanaoka,Mattia Sarno,Francesco Tortorella,Biwen Meng,Jingxin Liu,Sara Krauss,Daniel Hieber,Lavish Ramchandani,Dev Kumar Das,Mieko Ochi,Yuan Bae,Piotr Giedziun,Mateusz Maniewski,Vangala Govindakrishnan Saipradeep,Naveen Sivadasan,Leire Benito-Del-Valle,Adrian Galdran,Kaustubh Atey,Sameer Anand Jha,Adinath Dukre,Imran Razzak,Maxime W. Lafarge,Viktor H. Koelzer,Nils Porsche,Nikolas Stathonikos,Mitko Veta,Dominik Hirling,Zsanett Zsófia Iván,Peter Horvath,Katharina Breininger,Christof A. Bertram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIDOG 2025挑战赛评估了有丝分裂检测算法在前所未有的生物学和上下文多样性下的泛化能力，揭示了当前模型在"真实世界"场景中仍面临显著挑战，尤其在困难区域和罕见肿瘤类型上性能明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的有丝分裂检测基准主要关注扫描仪引起的域偏移，但临床"真实世界"应用要求模型在组织学多样性（包括不同物种、肿瘤类型、扫描平台、不同组织区域类型）中保持鲁棒性。现有评估方法仅基于人工选择的热点区域，无法真实反映全切片检测的临床需求，因此需要一个更贴近临床实际的多上下文评估框架。

Method: 构建了包含365例样本的测试数据集，涵盖12种人、犬、猫肿瘤类型，使用多种扫描平台数字化。评估不仅包含传统热点区域，还包括随机组织区域（模拟全切片检测场景）和富含困难负样本的挑战区域。设置两个赛道：第一赛道为有丝分裂检测（18支参赛队伍），第二赛道引入非典型有丝分裂象（AMF）分类任务（21份提交）。同时评估了集成学习（ensembling）和测试时增强（TTA）的效果。

Result: 检测赛道最高F1分数达0.740，AMF分类赛道最高平衡准确率达0.908。大多数模型在传统热点区域表现可靠，但在挑战性ROI中假阳性率增加了三倍，性能显著下降。不同肿瘤类型间性能差异显著，暴露了当前最优架构在罕见或高度多形性恶性肿瘤上的"盲区"。集成学习分别带来F1分数和平衡准确率平均1.5和1.3个百分点的提升，而TTA未带来明显改善。

Conclusion: 野外"有丝分裂检测仍是重大挑战。从仅评估热点区域到多上下文评估框架的转变，为衡量临床可靠性提供了更真实的代理指标。当前模型在面对生物学和上下文多样性时仍存在明显不足，尤其在困难区域和罕见肿瘤类型上的泛化能力亟需提升。"

Abstract: Automated mitosis detection is a well-established task in computational pathology. While previous benchmarks focused on scanner-induced domain shift, clinical "real-world" application requires models to be robust across the vast variance to be expected in the histological landscape. The MItosis DOmain Generalization (MIDOG) 2025 challenge was designed to evaluate algorithmic performance across unprecedented biological and contextual diversity. We curated a test dataset of 365 cases, encompassing 12 distinct human, canine and feline tumor types, digitized across multiple scanning platforms. Moving beyond hand-selected hotspots, the challenge required detection also in random tissue areas (representative of the whole slide detection situation) and challenging areas (areas rich in hard negatives). In the second track, we introduced the classification of atypical mitotic figures (AMFs). There were 18 teams submitting to the detection track, with F1 scores ranging up to 0.740. In the AMF detection track, we had 21 submissions with balanced accuracy values up to 0.908. Our analysis reveals that while most models perform reliably in traditional hotspots, significant performance degradation occurs in challenging ROIs, where false positive rates tripled. Furthermore, performance varied significantly across the 12 tumor types, highlighting "blind spots" in current state-of-the-art architectures when encountering rare or highly pleomorphic malignancies. Moreover, we evaluated the effectiveness of ensembling and found a mean increases of 1.5 and 1.3 percentage points in F1 score and balanced accuracy, respectively. In contrast, TTA showed no relevant improvement. MIDOG 2025 demonstrates that "in the wild" mitosis detection remains a significant hurdle. The transition from hotspot-only evaluation to a multi-contextual framework provides a more realistic proxy for clinical reliability.

</details>


### [81] [Mind the Gap: Disentangling Performance Bottlenecks in Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07394)
*Danial Hamdi,Fardin Ayar,Mahdi Javanmardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于整数线性规划（ILP）的诊断框架，用于分层隔离视频实例分割（VIS）中分类、分割和跟踪各自的误差贡献，揭示跟踪不稳定性是核心瓶颈，并开发可视化工具TrackLens辅助定位具体失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频实例分割（VIS）联合评估分类、分割和跟踪三个目标，但各子任务对整体性能损失的具体贡献不透明，缺乏系统性手段来隔离和量化各误差来源，导致难以针对性地改进模型。

Method: 提出一个与模型无关的诊断框架：将身份分配和类别分配建模为整数线性规划（ILP）问题，构建层次化的oracle，逐步隔离跟踪、分类和分割各自的误差贡献。该框架应用于七种涵盖在线和离线范式的VIS方法，在YouTube-VIS 2019/2021和OVIS诊断子集上进行分析。此外，引入TrackLens可视化工具，将AP差距转化为可观测的、查询级别的失败模式。

Result: （1）跟踪不稳定性是在线方法的关键瓶颈，在严重遮挡场景下AP差距超过20；（2）跟踪误差随视频长度和实例密度增加而急剧增长；（3）语义分类在标准基准上贡献显著，但在跟踪严重失败的场景中影响可忽略；（4）更强的骨干网络能大幅提升默认分数，但跟踪AP差距基本不变，证实时序脆弱性本质上是算法问题而非纯表征能力问题。

Conclusion: 跟踪的时序关联鲁棒性是VIS的核心挑战，其瓶颈本质是算法层面的而非表征层面的。所提出的ILP诊断框架和TrackLens工具为系统性定位和解决VIS中的误差来源提供了基础，特别是为改善长期鲁棒时序关联指明了方向。

Abstract: In Video Instance Segmentation (VIS), classification, segmentation, and tracking objectives are jointly evaluated, but their individual contributions to performance loss remain opaque. We introduce a diagnostic framework that formulates identity and class assignment as an Integer Linear Program (ILP), yielding a model-agnostic oracle that hierarchically isolates each error source. Applied to seven VIS methods spanning online and offline paradigms across YouTube-VIS 2019/2021 and a diagnostic subset of OVIS, our analysis reveals a consistent picture. Tracking instability is a critical bottleneck for online methods, with gaps exceeding 20 AP under heavy occlusion, and grows sharply with video length and instance density. While semantic classification contributes meaningfully on standard benchmarks, its impact becomes negligible where tracking fails most. Although stronger backbones substantially lift default scores, they leave AP tracking gaps largely intact, confirming that temporal fragility is algorithmic rather than purely representational. To complement the oracle, we introduce TrackLens, a visual tool that translates gap magnitude into observable, query-level failure modes. Together, these tools provide a systematic foundation for targeting VIS's core challenge: robust long-term temporal association.

</details>


### [82] [RealDocBench: A Benchmark for Field-Level QA and Layout Understanding on Real-World Regulated Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07401)
*Ameya Joshi,Joon Kim,Gus Eggert,Joseph Bajor,Cindy Hao,Jing Reyhan,Kushal Byatnal,Eli Badgio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealDocBench是一个基于真实受监管文档构建的双轨道基准测试，包含QA轨道（1,356个字段级问题/581份文档/四个领域）和布局轨道（1,500张标注页面图像），用于评估文档解析系统在实际场景中的字段级准确性、成本和延迟表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准测试主要基于干净的学术排版或合成文本进行评估，仅报告单一的OCR或markdown相似度分数，这与下游代理在真实复杂页面上提取特定字段正确值的实际需求严重脱节。在抵押贷款审批、财务报告、供应链物流和临床记录等高风险受监管工作流中，缺乏能反映真实表现的字段级评估基准。

Method: 提出RealDocBench双轨道基准：(1) QA轨道包含581份真实受监管文档上的1,356个字段级问题，每个问题配有类型化的gold_dict键值对答案，使用逐字段和严格逐问题准确率评分；(2) 布局轨道包含1,500张人工验证的页面图像，采用COCO风格边界框标注（九类公开分类体系），使用带有邻接感知分裂/合并恢复的匈牙利匹配器评分。在统一的提取与评分协议下评估18个系统（商业解析API、通用VLM、开源OCR模型），同时报告准确率、每页成本和防缓存延迟。

Result: 评估的18个系统展现出被单一数值基准所掩盖的巨大性能差异；医疗子领域持续表现困难；不同操作点之间存在显著的成本/延迟权衡。

Conclusion: RealDocBench揭示了现有单一数值基准无法反映的文档解析系统真实性能差异，尤其在医疗等困难领域。该基准通过字段级评估、真实受监管文档和多维度指标（准确率、成本、延迟），为文档解析系统提供了更贴近实际应用的可复现比较框架。数据集、解析器适配器和评估工具已公开发布。

Abstract: Document parsing systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes, regulated workflows such as mortgage underwriting, financial reporting, supply-chain logistics, and clinical records. Yet most public benchmarks evaluate parsers on clean academic layouts or synthetic prose, and report a single OCR or markdown-level similarity score. Such documents and metrics correlate poorly with what downstream agents actually need: the correct value for a specific field on a messy real-world page. We introduce RealDocBench, a two-track benchmark built from real regulated documents. The QA track contains 1,356 field-level questions over 581 documents spanning four domains, where each question is paired with a typed gold_dict of key-to-value answers and parsers are scored on both per-field and strict per-question accuracy. The layout track contains 1,500 human-verified page images annotated with COCO-style bounding boxes under a nine-class public taxonomy, scored with a Hungarian matcher that includes adjacency-aware split/merge recovery. We evaluate eighteen systems, spanning commercial parsing APIs, general-purpose VLMs, and open-source OCR models, under a uniform extraction-and-scoring protocol, and report accuracy alongside per-page cost and cache-busted latency. RealDocBench exposes a wide performance spread that single-number benchmarks hide, a persistently hard medical sub-domain, and sharp cost/latency trade-offs across operating points. We release the datasets, parser adapters, and evaluation harness to support reproducible, field-level comparison of document parsing systems.

</details>


### [83] [DisPOSE: Projected Polystochastic Diffusion for Self-Supervised Multi-View 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07419)
*Tony Danjun Wang,Tolga Birdal,Nassir Navab,Lennart Bastian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisPOSE是一个自监督框架，将多视角多人3D姿态估计中的人员分配问题建模为多随机张量空间上的扩散生成过程，结合超图卷积解码器回归完整3D骨架，在标准数据集和高遮挡手术室场景中均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角多人3D人体姿态恢复是行为分析的基础瓶颈。现有自监督方法依赖合成3D姿态目录，由于分布偏移导致在真实场景中泛化能力差。此外，多视角人员匹配本质上是一个离散组合优化问题，难以端到端优化。因此需要一种不依赖合成数据、能有效解决跨视角人员分配并适应不同相机配置的自监督方法。

Method: 提出DisPOSE框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) **扩散式人员分配模块**：将离散的多视角人员分配问题近似为多随机张量（polystochastic tensor）空间上的生成扩散过程，在去噪过程中使用可微的Sinkhorn投影，基于2D图像先验引导解向有效可行的分配方案；(2) **超图卷积解码器（Hypergraph-Convolutional Decoder）**：对定位后的个体，显式建模多视角间的关系结构和关节铰接关系，回归完整的3D骨架。整个框架保持可微性，将分配与根节点回归解耦。

Result: DisPOSE在标准数据集上超越了当前最先进的自监督方法；在新提出的高遮挡手术室场景基准上也展现了强劲性能；扩散式定位具有高标签效率，仅使用10%的伪标签即可保留99%的性能；由于分配与根节点回归的解耦设计，该方法对不同相机配置几乎不敏感。

Conclusion: DisPOSE通过将多视角人员分配问题创新性地建模为多随机张量上的扩散过程，结合可微Sinkhorn投影和超图卷积解码器，实现了高效的自监督多人3D姿态估计。该方法不仅在性能上超越现有自监督方法，还具备高标签效率和对相机配置的强鲁棒性，在高遮挡的真实临床场景中也表现出色，展示了广泛的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human poses for multiple individuals from different camera views is a fundamental bottleneck for analyzing interacting behaviors. Existing self-supervised approaches leverage synthetic catalogues of 3D poses; however, this leads to poor generalization in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts. We therefore introduce DisPOSE, a self-supervised framework that approximates the inherently discrete multi-view person-assignment problem as a generative diffusion process over the space of polystochastic tensors. By employing differentiable Sinkhorn projections during denoising, our model learns to guide solutions toward valid and feasible assignments based on 2D image priors. The complete 3D skeletons of localized individuals are then regressed using a Hypergraph-Convolutional Decoder that explicitly models relational structures and articulated joints across multiple views. The proposed approach outperforms current state-of-the-art self-supervised methods on standard datasets and demonstrates strong performance on a newly proposed benchmark featuring highly occluded scenes from surgical operating rooms. Our diffusion-based localization demonstrates high label efficiency, retaining 99% of its performance with only 10% of the pseudo-labels. Notably, disentangling the assignment and root regression components while maintaining differentiability makes DisPOSE nearly agnostic to different camera arrangements.

</details>


### [84] [OpenGlass: Ultra-Low-Power On-Device AI Eyewear with Event-based Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07431)
*Pietro Bonazzi,Julian Moosmann,Ahmet Celik,Philipp Mayer,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源智能眼镜平台，采用模块化设计支持事件相机和帧相机，通过硬件-软件协同设计的电源管理实现200mAh电池下11.5小时的持续端侧ML推理，并在自中心手势识别任务上验证了平台有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜受限于紧凑外形下的功耗、内存和计算约束，而支持事件驱动视觉和嵌入式ML的开放硬件平台极为稀缺，缺乏用于快速原型验证新型传感器和算法的开源研究平台。

Method: 1) 模块化硬件设计：使用柔性FPC中间板支持事件相机和帧相机的热插拔，无需重新设计PCB；2) 硬件-软件协同电源管理：结合可配置PMIC与nRF5340协调器的事件驱动唤醒机制，在推理间隔期间关闭GAP9 RISC-V SoC；3) 手势识别流水线：使用Prophesee GENX320事件相机生成极性分离事件直方图，采用R(2+1)D网络进行自中心手势识别，并应用时间增强和移除歧义类别策略提升性能。

Result: 平台在200mAh电池下实现最长11.5小时连续端侧ML推理；在LynX数据集上采用留二被试交叉验证，R(2+1)D模型达到最佳跨被试准确率83.94%（宏F1=0.781）；GAP9上端到端推理延迟为78.3ms；时间增强和移除歧义类别带来最大性能提升（+8.9个百分点）。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了一个开源、模块化的智能眼镜研究平台，在极低功耗下实现了事件驱动视觉与嵌入式ML的有效结合，验证了其在自中心手势识别场景中的实用性，所有硬件设计、固件和模型均已开源发布，为该领域的快速原型开发提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Smart eyewear enables unobtrusive, context-aware interaction through multimodal sensors and on-device intelligence, but is severely limited by power, memory, and compute constraints in a compact form factor. Open-hardware platforms supporting event-based vision and embedded ML at this scale are rare. This work introduces an open-source smart glasses platform for rapid prototyping of novel sensors and algorithms. Its modular design uses a flexible FPC interposer to support both event-based and frame-based cameras without full PCB redesign. A hardware-software co-designed power management system combines a configurable PMIC with event-driven wake-up via an nRF5340 coordinator, keeping the GAP9 RISC-V SoC powered down between inferences. The prototype achieves up to 11.5 hours of continuous on-device ML from a 200 mAh battery. As a demonstration, an egocentric hand gesture recognition pipeline was evaluated on the LynX dataset using polarity-separated event histograms from a Prophesee GENX320 camera. R(2+1)D achieved the best cross-subject accuracy of 83.94\% (macro F1 = 0.781) under leave-two-subjects-out validation, with 78.3 ms end-to-end inference latency on the GAP9. Temporal augmentation and removal of ambiguous classes provided the largest gains (+8.9 pp). All hardware designs, firmware, and models are released open source.

</details>


### [85] [Watch, Remember, Reason: Human-View Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07433)
*Jiahao Meng,Yue Tan,Qi Xu,Kuan Gao,Weisong Liu,Yanwei Li,Jason Li,Lingdong Kong,Haochen Wang,Qianyu Zhou,Jiangning Zhang,Guangliang Cheng,Yunhai Tong,Lu Qi,Minghsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从人类认知视角出发，提出以"观看(Watching)、记忆(Remembering)、推理(Reasoning)"三大功能能力为框架，对基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的视频理解领域进行系统性综述，涵盖方法、应用、数据集与评估基准，并展望未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解正从短片段快速扩展到长视频、多模态、知识密集型场景，这要求模型处理稀疏证据、长程依赖、多模态对齐和有限算力下的可靠推理。然而，现有研究通常将视频任务视为孤立的基准测试，缺乏统一的分析框架。本文旨在从人类认知（观看、记忆、推理）的角度提供一个统一结构，系统性地分析视频MLLM如何获取证据、保持上下文并生成有据可依的输出。

Method: 本文提出了一个以人类视角为核心的分析框架，将视频理解系统形式化为感知表征(perceptual representations)、记忆状态(memory states)、推理轨迹(reasoning traces)和最终预测(final predictions)四个组成部分。围绕三大能力组织代表性方法：(1) **观看(Watching)**：涵盖细粒度感知、全面感知、视听感知和高效感知；(2) **记忆(Remembering)**：包括离线记忆和流式记忆建模；(3) **推理(Reasoning)**：包括纯文本推理和结合视频的思考推理。同时系统梳理了自我中心视频、体育、教学、医学和叙事视频等应用领域，以及训练数据集和评估基准。

Result: 本文系统识别了视频MLLM面临的核心挑战，包括时空感知、长视频高效处理、记忆建模、流式理解和忠实推理等方面。通过对大量代表性方法的梳理，揭示了当前各方法在观看、记忆和推理三个维度上的能力覆盖情况，并全面覆盖了不同任务类型、监督格式、模态和能力维度下的评估基准。

Conclusion: 本文为基于LLM的视频理解提供了一个统一的人类认知视角分析框架，强调观看、记忆和推理三大能力的协同作用。未来方向指向可扩展的(scalable)、具有记忆感知能力的(memory-aware)和证据驱动的(evidence-grounded)视频智能系统，相关工作持续在GitHub仓库中追踪更新。

Abstract: Video understanding is being rapidly transformed by multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as research moves from short clips to long, multimodal, and knowledge-intensive video scenarios. These scenarios require models to handle sparse evidence, long-range dependencies, multimodal alignment, and reliable inference under limited computational budgets. This work presents a human-view perspective on LLM-based video understanding, organized around three functional abilities: watching, remembering, and reasoning. Rather than treating video tasks as isolated benchmarks, this view provides a unified structure for analyzing how video MLLMs acquire evidence, preserve context, and produce grounded outputs. We introduce a formulation that characterizes video understanding systems by their perceptual representations, memory states, reasoning traces, and final predictions. Based on this formulation, we identify challenges in spatio-temporal perception, efficient long-video processing, memory modeling, streaming understanding, and faithful reasoning. Representative methods are organized by their roles in video MLLM systems. Watching covers fine-grained, comprehensive, audio-visual, and efficient perception. Remembering includes offline and streaming memory, while reasoning covers text-only reasoning and thinking with videos. We further examine application domains such as egocentric, sports, instructional, medical, and narrative videos, and cover training datasets and evaluation benchmarks across task types, supervision formats, modalities, and capability dimensions. Finally, we outline open problems and future directions for scalable, memory-aware, and evidence-grounded video intelligence. Related works will be continuously traced at https://github.com/marinero4972/Awesome-HumanView-VideoUnderstanding.

</details>


### [86] [Skill-3D: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic 3D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07436)
*Haoyuan Li,Zhengdong Hu,Jun Wang,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Skill-3D 提出了一种自进化的场景感知技能框架，通过场景记忆和技能库的协同演化，解决了多模态大语言模型智能体在3D空间推理中工具误用和工具偏好偏差的问题，显著提升了工具利用率和3D空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM智能体在3D场景下存在工具误用和工具偏好偏差问题，智能体范式相比非智能体策略仅有微弱提升。核心原因在于3D空间推理任务在不同场景间具有异质性，但现有智能体对所有场景采用统一的工具使用策略，而非根据具体场景和任务选择合适的工具。

Method: 提出Skill-3D框架，学习自进化的场景感知技能：(1) 识别任务场景并将智能体的工具使用轨迹记录到场景记忆(Scene Memory)中；(2) 将相似场景中成功的轨迹聚合蒸馏为可复用的场景感知技能，失败轨迹作为经验教训附加到技能中；(3) 训练时，当相似场景再次出现时，注入对应技能引导智能体，产生新的轨迹进一步优化技能，形成记忆与技能库协同进化的闭环；(4) 在技能引导的轨迹上进行智能体后训练(agentic post-training)。

Result: Skill-3D在VSI-Bench上将工具利用率从39%提升至78%；在MMSI-Bench上将Gemini-3-Flash性能提升67%；通过对技能引导轨迹的智能体后训练，Qwen3-VL-8B在VSI-Bench上性能提升43%。

Conclusion: Skill-3D通过场景感知的自进化技能学习机制，有效解决了3D空间推理中的工具误用问题，推动智能体实现正确且充分的工具使用，在多个基准测试上取得了显著的性能提升，验证了场景感知技能对3D智能体推理的重要性。

Abstract: This paper explores agentic 3D spatial understanding, i.e., MLLM agents performing 3D reasoning through tool use. Existing methods often misuse tools and exhibit biased tool preferences under 3D scenarios, leaving the agentic paradigm with only marginal gains over non-agentic strategies. We reveal that 3D spatial reasoning tasks are heterogeneous across scenes, while these agents apply a uniform tool-use strategy to all scenes rather than selecting tools according to the specific scene and task. To address this, we propose Skill-3D, a framework that learns self-evolving scene-aware skills. Specifically, Skill-3D identifies the task scene and records the agent's tool-use trajectory into a Scene Memory, where successful trajectories from similar scenes are aggregated and distilled into a reusable scene-aware skill, with failed ones attached to the skill as lessons. During training, once a similar scene recurs, the corresponding skill is injected to guide the agent, producing new trajectories whose successes and failures further refine the skill, forming a loop in which the memory and the skill library co-evolve. Experiments show that Skill-3D substantially improves tool utilization in 3D spatial reasoning (from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench), driving the agent toward correct and sufficient tool use. For instance, it improves Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench. Furthermore, we conduct agentic post-training over skill-guided trajectories, which boosts Qwen3-VL-8B by 43% on VSI-Bench.

</details>


### [87] [Implicit Data Synthesis for Contrastive Unsupervised Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07498)
*Patrick Kage,Trevor Hedges,N. Siddharth,Pavlos Andreadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种通过扰动网络权重（而非数据增强）来生成对比样本的对比学习方法，适用于科学观测数据，并在流星雷达观测数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学观测产生大量无标签数据，人工标注成本高昂，对比学习虽能从无标注数据中提取结构化表征，但传统方法依赖数据空间的增强变换来生成对比样本。然而，对于科学观测数据，数据空间的扰动可能从根本上改变底层数据的物理含义和结构，使得传统数据增强策略不再适用。因此需要一种不依赖数据增强、同时能保留数据结构的对比学习方法。

Method: 提出通过扰动网络权重（而非底层数据）来生成对比样本的方法。具体而言，基于SimCLR框架，不再对输入数据施加数据空间的增强操作，而是通过对网络权重进行扰动来产生不同的表征视图，从而构建对比学习所需的正样本对。这种方式能更好地保留原始科学数据的结构完整性。

Result: 在流星雷达观测数据上应用该方法，在匹配的实验协议下，相比传统数据增强方式的对比学习方法取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 对于科学观测数据等数据增强可能破坏数据物理意义的场景，通过扰动网络权重生成对比样本是一种有效的替代方案，能够在保留数据结构的同时实现无监督表征学习，具有在科学数据处理中的应用价值。

Abstract: Scientific observations generate large quantities of unlabeled data which is laborious to hand-label, making unsupervised learning techniques valuable for processing datasets. Among these approaches, contrastive learning provides a convenient mechanism for extracting structural representations from unannotated datasets. For natural imagery, the general approach is to use a variety of data-space augmentation methods in order to generate synthetic samples; however, for scientific observations data-space perturbations can fundamentally alter the underlying data. Our proposed method is to generate contrastive samples by perturbing the network weights rather than the underlying data, thus more closely preserving the structure of the data. We demonstrate this technique using a SimCLR-based pipeline applied over radar observations of meteors, and show performance gains under matched protocols.

</details>


### [88] [Differences in Detection: Explainability Where it Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07503)
*Johannes Theodoridis,Johannes Maucher,Andreas Schilling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DnD（Differences in Detection）的直观方法，通过计算两个目标检测模型在ground truth标签上的交集、差集和补集来直接比较两个模型的检测差异，并可结合错误类型在混淆矩阵中进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标检测评估指标（如mAP和TIDE错误分析）只能独立地总结单个模型的性能，无法直接比较两个模型之间的具体差异。通过独立的汇总统计量进行比较不够直观，难以揭示模型各自独有的和共同的错误。

Method: 基于相同的匹配算法，计算两个模型都正确识别的ground truth标签的交集、各自独有识别的差集、以及两个模型都遗漏的补集。将检测差异与错误类型结合后，可以自然地在标准混淆矩阵中分析检测错误的差异。该方法作为mAP和TIDE错误分析的补充工具。

Result: DnD能够揭示两个模型各自独有的和共同的错误，提供比独立汇总统计量更直观的模型比较。结合错误类型时，可通过混淆矩阵自然地分析检测错误差异。此外，DnD可以引导ODAM等可解释性方法关注与指标相关的、基于结构化子集的样例。

Conclusion: DnD提供了一种直观且结构化的方式来直接比较两个目标检测模型，弥补了现有独立评估指标的不足。其最佳应用之一是引导可解释性方法（如ODAM）聚焦于基于结构化子集的、与指标相关的样例，从而增强模型分析的深度。

Abstract: We propose Differences in Detection (DnD), an intuitive method to compare two object detection models. Based on the same matching algorithm, it complements the standard metrics of mean Average Precision ($mAP$) and TIDE error analysis with the ability to compare two models directly. More specifically, we calculate the intersection of ground truth labels that are recognized by both models, followed by the corresponding difference sets and the complement set of ground truth labels that are missed by both models. The resulting comparison is more direct and intuitive than a comparison of independent summary statistics. It reveals individual and shared mistakes and becomes particularly interesting when combined with error types. In this case, the differences in detection errors can be analyzed naturally in a standard confusion matrix. While valuable in itself, we believe that one of the best applications of DnD is to guide explainability methods such as ODAM towards metric-relevant examples, grounded in structured subsets. The code for our method is available here: https://github.com/JohannesTheo/differences-in-detection

</details>


### [89] [Streaming Video Generation with Streaming Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07508)
*Hanhui Wang,Yiming Xie,Haiwen Feng,Zhaoyang Lv,Shenlong Wang,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamForce是一个流式视频生成框架，通过统一的力表示实现物理驱动的连续力控制，支持因果推理，在单GPU上可达16.6 FPS，同时保证力响应的准确性和运动真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于力控制的视频生成模型存在多种局限：需要针对不同力类型训练独立模型、假设力为固定不变、依赖非因果处理方式，无法实时响应连续变化的力输入。研究者希望构建一个统一的、因果的流式模型，能够即时且连贯地响应局部和全局的时变力信号。

Method: StreamForce提出三项关键设计：（1）设计了统一的力表示（unified force representation）作为控制信号，将局部力和全局力统一编码；（2）开发了面向力可控视频生成的蒸馏管线（distillation pipeline），实现高效的力控制能力迁移；（3）采用因果自回归架构，将自回归生成的高效性与力响应能力相结合，支持流式推理，保持稳定的光度质量和动态真实性。

Result: StreamForce在单GPU上运行速度可达16.6 FPS，在力遵循性（force adherence）和运动真实性（motion realism）两方面均达到了最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: StreamForce成功实现了一个统一的、因果的流式视频生成框架，能够实时响应连续时变的物理力输入，兼顾生成效率与物理真实性，为物理驱动的交互式视频生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce StreamForce, a streaming video generation framework that enables physically grounded control through continuous force inputs. Unlike prior video models that train separate models for different force types, assume fixed forces, or rely on non-causal processing, StreamForce is a causal and unified model that responds instantly and coherently to both local and global, time-varying forces. To achieve this, we design a unified force representation as a control signal and develop a distillation pipeline for force-controllable video generation. Our model combines autoregressive efficiency with force responsiveness, sustaining stable photometric and dynamic realism. StreamForce runs at up to 16.6 FPS on a single GPU, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both force adherence and motion realism. Project website: https://neu-vi.github.io/StreamForce/

</details>


### [90] [UniSHARP: Universal Sharp Monocular View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07514)
*Meixi Song,Dizhe Zhang,Hao Ren,Ruiyang Zhang,Bo Du,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSHARP将SHARP方法扩展至通用单目渲染，通过统一的全景潜空间对齐不同相机系统的图像，实现从透视相机到鱼眼、全景等多种成像系统的逼真视图合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHARP作为流行的逼真视图合成方法，受限于针孔相机的假设，无法直接适用于广角、鱼眼和全景等多种相机系统。现有方法缺乏对不同视场角相机系统的统一处理能力，因此需要一种能够跨越连续相机系统谱系（从传统透视相机到全景相机）进行通用单目渲染的方法。

Method: 提出UniSHARP，核心思想是将不同相机系统的图像对齐到统一的全景潜空间。具体包括：(1) 在特征空间和高斯空间中进行隐式对齐；(2) 采用基于射线的通用表示，沿射线方向和径向距离排列高斯基元；(3) 利用受UniK3D启发的编码器提取2D语义特征和3D空间特征，联合解码生成完整的高斯点云。此外，构建了一个覆盖多种成像系统和多种场景的基准测试集，并按视场角（FoV）分层以实现对通用单目渲染任务的细粒度评估。

Result: 在所构建的基准测试集上进行的大量实验表明，UniSHARP在通用单目渲染任务中表现优异，以显著优势超越了其他对比方法。

Conclusion: UniSHARP通过统一全景潜空间对齐策略和基于射线的高斯表示，成功将SHARP扩展为适用于从透视到全景等连续相机系统的通用单目渲染框架，在多样化成像系统的基准测试中展现了显著的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on extending SHARP, the popular photorealistic view synthesis method, for universal monocular rendering across a continuum of camera systems, from conventional perspective cameras to wide-field-of-view, fisheye and omnidirectional panoramic settings. To overcome the pinhole-specific assumptions of SHARP, our key idea is to align various images in a unified omnidirectional latent space. Thus, we propose UniSHARP, which performs implicit alignment in both feature and Gaussian spaces. Specifically, Gaussian primitives are arranged along rays and radial distances in a ray-based universal representation, while 2D semantic and 3D spatial features extracted from UniK3D-inspired encoders are jointly decoded to generate the complete Gaussian cloud. To comprehensively evaluate our method, we construct a benchmark covering diverse imaging systems across various scenes. The benchmark is further stratified by field of view (FoV) to enable fine-grained assessment of the universal monocular rendering task. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of UniSHARP, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin. The project page can be found at: https://insta360-research-team.github.io/Unisharp-website/

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

### [91] [Improving Cross-Lingual Factual Recall via Consistency-Driven Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06586)
*Jonathan von Rad,Louis Arts,George Burgess,Eleftheria Kolokytha,Harry O'Donnell,Ektor Oikonomidis Doumpas,Eduardo Sanchez,Yao Lu,Pontus Stenetorp*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PolyFact——一个包含12种语言、10万条Wikidata事实的大规模平行多语言事实QA数据集，并发现GRPO强化学习方法在提升跨语言事实一致性方面优于SFT和持续预训练，其机制在于减少MLP层和注意力头的语言专门化，促进跨语言共享表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型主要在英语数据上训练，虽编码了大量世界知识，但在其他语言中常无法可靠表达这些知识，即跨语言事实不一致问题。现有研究缺乏大规模、类型学多样的平行多语言事实数据集来系统研究和解决该问题。

Method: 1) 构建PolyFact数据集：基于Wikidata，覆盖12种类型学多样语言的10万条平行事实QA对；2) 在Qwen-2.5-7B和OLMo-2-1124-7B上系统比较三种方法：轻量持续预训练（CPT）、监督微调（SFT）和基于群体相对策略优化的强化学习（GRPO）；3) 进行机制分析，探究GRPO如何重组多语言路由机制。

Result: GRPO在跨语言事实一致性和对未见语言的泛化能力上均优于SFT；平行数据上的持续预训练带来的额外增益有限；机制分析表明GRPO通过减少MLP层和注意力头中的语言专门化，重组了多语言路由，促进了更多跨语言共享表示的形成。

Conclusion: GRPO是提升LLM跨语言事实一致性的有效方法，其优势不仅体现在性能上，更在于其能从机制层面促进跨语言知识共享。作者开源了代码、模型和数据集，为多语言LLM研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained predominantly on English data encode substantial world knowledge, yet often fail to express it reliably in other languages, a phenomenon known as cross-lingual factual inconsistency. To study and address this, we introduce PolyFact, a large-scale parallel multilingual factual QA dataset containing 100K Wikidata-grounded facts across 12 typologically diverse languages. Using PolyFact, we compare light continual pretraining (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for improving cross-lingual factual recall in Qwen-2.5-7B and OLMo-2-1124-7B. We find that GRPO consistently outperforms SFT, improving both cross-lingual consistency and generalization to unseen languages, while CPT on parallel data yields limited additional gains. Mechanistic analyses further show that GRPO reorganizes multilingual routing by reducing language specialization in MLP layers and attention heads, thereby promoting more shared cross-lingual representations. We release our code, models, and dataset.

</details>


### [92] [Re-Centering Humans in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06614)
*Lechen Zhang,Jiarui Liu,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM个性化系统在使用合成数据与真实人类数据时的性能差距，发现现有系统在个性化三个阶段均存在局限，并提出轻量级训练干预方法来缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM个性化能力的评估大多依赖合成数据，尚不清楚现有个性化系统在真实用户场景下的表现如何。研究者希望揭示合成数据评估与真实人类评估之间的差距，从而推动构建真正符合人类需求的个性化系统。

Method: 研究者将个性化分解为三个阶段进行系统评估：（1）从对话中提取用户属性（收集550段人类对话，5,949条人类判断）；（2）将相关属性与新提示进行匹配（11,919条判断）；（3）将相关属性融入个性化回复（1,101条判断）。在此基础上，针对前两个阶段提出了两种轻量级的基于训练的干预方法，使自动化评估更接近人类数据；在第三阶段训练了奖励模型来预测人类偏好。

Result: （1）模型难以从真实人类对话中准确提取用户属性；（2）模型在判断属性相关性方面与人类判断存在显著分歧；（3）个性化回复在人类评判中并不优于通用回复，但LLM评判器却普遍认为个性化回复更好，揭示了LLM评判与人类评判的显著偏差；（4）轻量级训练干预在前两个阶段有效缩小了自动评估与人类评估的差距；（5）在第三阶段，学习的奖励模型与人类评分仅达到中等相关性，表明个性化质量判断难以直接建模。

Conclusion: 当前LLM个性化系统在真实用户场景下的表现远不如合成数据评估所示，三个阶段均暴露出明显不足。LLM评判器与人类判断之间存在系统性偏差，尤其在个性化回复质量评估上。所收集的人类数据为未来研究如何以符合人类真实需求的方式提取、选择和整合用户信息提供了基础。

Abstract: Despite growing interest, most evaluations of large language models' (LLMs') personalization abilities have relied on synthetic data. It remains unclear how well current personalization systems work for real users. In this paper, we study the gap in LLM personalization performance when using synthetic versus human data. We collect human conversations (550 conversations) and judgments across three stages of personalization: extracting user attributes from conversations (5,949 judgments), pairing relevant attributes with new prompts (11,919), and incorporating relevant attributes into a personalized response (1,101). Incorporating human data reveals system limitations at each stage. Models struggle to extract attributes from human conversations, disagree with human judgments on relevant attributes, and generate personalized responses that humans judge no better than generic responses (though that LLM judges widely rate as better). We introduce two lightweight training-based interventions that shift automated personalization evaluation closer to human data in our first two stages. However, in our third stage we find that learned reward models achieve only modest correlation with human ratings, suggesting that human-aligned personalization quality judgments are difficult to model directly. Our collected data provides a foundation for studying how models should extract, select, and incorporate user information in ways that humans find useful.

</details>


### [93] [UnpredictaBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Distributional Randomness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06622)
*Amirhossein Abaskohi,Amirhossein Dabiriaghdam,Liang Luo,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Lele Wang,Giuseppe Carenini,Peter West*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UnpredictaBench基准，用于评估大语言模型捕捉真实底层分布的能力，发现当前模型在分布采样任务上表现有限，即使是简单的分布模拟仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益被用作其他实体的替代品（如经济模拟中替代人类），许多模型倾向于坍缩到单一合理答案，无法捕捉真实系统的不可预测性。现有提升输出多样性的方法不足以解决该问题，因为模拟需要的是与目标分布校准的样本，而非仅仅多样化的输出。目前缺乏一个系统性的基准来评估LLM的分布采样能力。

Method: 提出UnpredictaBench评估基准，包含448个问题，涵盖三类目标分布：经典统计分布、随机程序诱导的分布、以及描述随机过程的自然语言场景。同时引入KS@N评估指标，基于Kolmogorov-Smirnov统计检验量化模型输出对黑箱目标分布的近似程度——即模型生成的大小为N的样本与真实样本之间未能拒绝原假设的比率，N越大难度越高。

Result: 在开源和闭源模型上的测试显示，不同模型的分布采样能力差异显著。在标准指标KS@100下，模型得分从接近0到超过20%不等，没有任何模型能超过40%。引入推理（reasoning）可以在一定程度上提升分数，但并未从根本上解决问题。

Conclusion: 即使是简单的分布模拟对当前LLM仍然极具挑战性，UnpredictaBench揭示了模型在分布采样能力上的巨大提升空间。该基准是将LLM用作复杂系统替代品的必要前提步骤，为未来改进LLM的分布校准能力提供了明确的评估框架。

Abstract: We introduce UnpredictaBench, an evaluation that tests the ability of large language models (LLMs) to capture true underlying distributions. As LLMs are increasingly used as substitutes for other entities (e.g., for humans in economic simulations), the tendency of many models to collapse towards a single plausible answer means a failure to capture the unpredictability of real systems. Recent work on improving output diversity is insufficient for this setting: simulation requires samples that are calibrated to a target distribution, not merely varied outputs. UnpredictaBench isolates a simplified but fundamental version of this problem: sampling outcomes from individual target distributions, including canonical statistical distributions, distributions induced by stochastic programs, and natural-language scenarios that describe random processes. We introduce 448 such problems together with KS@N, a general-purpose evaluation metric that quantifies how well a model outputs approximate black-box target distributions via the Kolmogorov-Smirnov statistical test. This is the rate at which we fail to reject model samples of size N against ground-truth samples, with larger N indicating greater difficulty. Tested across open and proprietary models, we find a large spread in distributional capabilities. For instance, when models generate samples of size 100 (KS@100, our standard metric), scores range from near 0 to over 20%. No model is able to achieve over 40% at KS@100, showing significant headroom in distributional sampling as a capability. Although adding reasoning can somewhat increase scores, we find no immediate solution for this issue. UnpredictaBench shows that even simple distributional simulation remains challenging, making it a necessary first step toward using LLMs as stand-ins for complex systems.

</details>


### [94] [How Language Models Fail: Token-Level Signatures of Committed and Persistent Reasoning Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06635)
*Tanvi Thoria,Kiana Jafari,Marc R. Schlichting,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过token级别的不确定性信号识别出语言模型推理失败的两种可区分模式——承诺性失败和持续性不确定性，并验证了该框架在23个模型-数据集配置中的可复现性及其对自一致性方法的实际指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的推理失败通过不同过程产生，且在推理轨迹中留下可识别的特征。现有方法缺乏对推理失败何时以及如何变得可检测的系统性理解，需要一个框架来刻画这些失败模式，以便针对性地调整检测策略。

Method: 利用token级别的不确定性信号来刻画推理失败，识别出两种经验上可区分的失败过程：（1）承诺性失败（committed failure）：模型在推理轨迹早期就锁定在错误路径上，存在一个"承诺点"，超过该点后考虑更多token反而会损害失败检测效果；（2）持续性不确定性（persistent uncertainty）：不确定性在整个推理过程中持续积累，需要完整轨迹才能最好地区分失败与成功的补全。该框架提出可证伪的预测，并在23个模型-数据集配置上进行验证。

Result: 两种失败特征在23个模型-数据集配置中具有可复现性，框架的可证伪预测在23个案例中的20个成立，远高于两种失败模式下的随机基线。此外，该框架对自一致性（self-consistency）方法具有直接指导意义，能够识别不确定性信号何时可以补充自一致性、以及何时可以选择性跳过自一致性。

Conclusion: 推理失败通过两种具有不同可检测性特征的过程产生，该框架为理解LLM推理失败何时变得可检测提供了基础，并为根据失败模式自适应地调整检测策略提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Failures in language model reasoning emerge through distinct processes that leave identifiable signatures in the reasoning trace. We characterize these failures using token-level uncertainty signals, finding they arise through two empirically distinguishable processes. The first is committed failure, in which a model locks onto an incorrect reasoning path early in its trace. A central diagnostic signature is the commitment point, beyond which considering additional tokens hurt rather than help failure detection. In the second, persistent uncertainty, uncertainty instead accumulates throughout, and the full trace is needed to best distinguish failing from successful completions. These signatures reproduce across 23 model-dataset configurations, with the framework's falsifiable predictions holding in 20 of 23 cases, well above chance across both failure modes. Finally, we demonstrate our failure mode framework has direct implications for self-consistency, identifying when uncertainty signals complement it and when it can be selectively skipped. These results offer a foundation for understanding when LLM reasoning failures become detectable and for adapting detection strategies accordingly.

</details>


### [95] [CAF-Gen: A Multi-Agent System for Enriching Argumentation Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06646)
*Jakub Bąba,Jarosław Chudziak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CAF-Gen，一个基于多智能体的自动化框架，通过迭代式Creator-Reviewer管道将浅层论证结构丰富为符合Carneades论证框架（CAF）的形式化论证模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前论证挖掘（AM）技术仅能识别基本的主张和前提，难以捕获高级论证模式（如Carneades论证框架）所需的更丰富结构信息，包括前提类型、证明标准和论证方案等特征。单次生成模型在结构稳定性方面也存在固有缺陷，无法可靠地将自然文本中的复杂推理形式化。

Method: 提出CAF-Gen多智能体框架，采用迭代式Creator-Reviewer管道：由Creator智能体生成CAF兼容的论证模型输出，再由Reviewer（批评）智能体对其进行验证以确保结构完整性。通过多轮迭代反馈循环，逐步将浅层论证结构丰富为包含前提类型、证明标准和论证方案等要素的完整CAF模型。

Result: 实验表明，迭代反馈循环显著提升了生成数据的质量，生成结果与原始标注具有较强的一致性，同时产生了结构更丰富的论证模型。多智能体系统有效克服了单次生成方法的结构不稳定性问题。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作系统能够克服单次生成的局限性，为形式化论证的自动建模提供了一种鲁棒的方法论，有效弥合了浅层论证挖掘与高级形式化论证框架之间的差距。

Abstract: Formalizing complex reasoning from natural text is one of the central challenges in computational linguistics. It requires systems to understand not just keywords but also the context and complex reasoning embedded in a text. Current Argument Mining (AM) techniques identify basic claims and premises, yet they often struggle to capture the richer structural information required by advanced schemas such as the Carneades Argumentation Framework (CAF), which incorporates features such as premise types, proof standards, and argument schemes. We address this limitation by introducing CAF-Gen, an automated multi-agent framework designed to enrich shallow argument structures into CAF-compliant argument models. By employing an iterative Creator-Reviewer pipeline, a creator agent's output is validated by a critical agent to ensure structural integrity. This multi-agent collaboration is crucial for mitigating the structural instability typical of single-pass generative models. Our experiments demonstrate that the iterative feedback loop improves the quality of the resulting data and achieves strong alignment with the original annotations, while producing structurally richer models. Our findings show that the multi-agent system can overcome the limitations of single-pass generation, providing a robust methodology for the automated modeling of formal argumentation.

</details>


### [96] [The Piggyback Hypothesis of Generalization: Explaining and Mitigating Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06667)
*Jiachen Zhao,Zhengxuan Wu,Aryaman Arora,Yiyou Sun,David Bau,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"搭便车假说"（Piggyback Hypothesis）来解释LLM微调后的涌现性失对齐（Emergent Misalignment）现象，即聊天模板token将微调行为"搭载"到域外查询上，并据此提出Token正则化微调方法（TReFT）来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在窄领域微调后会出现广泛的过度泛化现象，尤其是涌现性失对齐（EM）——在窄任务上微调会导致模型在语义无关的测试域上出现广泛的失对齐行为。这种现象的内在机制尚不清楚，需要理解其根因并提出有效的缓解方案，以实现更可控的微调。

Method: 提出"搭便车假说"：聊天模板中的前缀token（prefix tokens，即用户查询前的所有token）会将微调行为搭载到域外查询上。验证方式包括：(1) 对前缀进行微妙扰动即可恢复对齐；(2) 用未微调模型的前缀表示替换微调模型的前缀表示也可恢复对齐。基于该发现，提出Token正则化微调（TReFT），在训练过程中对特定token的表示进行正则化约束，防止前缀token编码过度泛化的行为信号，从而缓解EM问题。

Result: 在多个模型和多个EM诱导数据集上，TReFT在减少EM的同时保持了域内学习能力。在Llama-3.1-8B法律领域微调实验中，TReFT比使用对齐样本保留集的数据交错方法多减少33.5%的EM。在其他窄领域微调场景（弃权、工具使用、拒绝）中，TReFT平均减少54.3%的域外过度泛化，进一步验证了搭便车假说。

Conclusion: LLM可能以非预期的方式学习和泛化，共享的输入特征（如聊天模板前缀token）可以作为行为跨域传播的载体。TReFT提供了一条通向更受约束微调的路径，未来需要进一步研究共享输入特征如何搭载模型行为跨域泛化的机制。

Abstract: The mechanisms behind LLMs' broad over-generalization beyond training examples remain unclear. Emergent misalignment (EM) offers a striking case study: finetuning on narrow tasks induces broad misalignment to semantically-unrelated test domains. In this work, we propose the Piggyback Hypothesis: the chat-template tokens can piggyback the finetuned behaviour onto out-of-domain queries. We validate this hypothesis by showing that subtle perturbations to the prefix (tokens preceding all user queries), or patching the prefix representations with those from the unfinetuned model, can restore alignment without changing the user query. Building on this finding, we propose Token-Regularized Finetuning (TReFT), which regularizes specific token representations during training to mitigate EM. Across different models and multiple EM-inducing datasets, TReFT reduces EM while preserving in-domain learning. On Llama-3.1-8B finetuned on the legal domain, TReFT achieves 33.5% more EM reduction than data interleaving with a retain set of aligned examples. We further show that TReFT extends to other narrow-finetuning settings, including abstention, tool use, and refusal (off-topic generalization is reduced by 54.3% on average), supporting the Piggyback Hypothesis. Broadly, our work highlights that LLMs may learn and generalize in unintended ways and suggests a path toward more constrained finetuning. It also calls for further study of how shared input features can piggyback model behavior across domains.

</details>


### [97] [What Do People Actually Want From AI? Mapping Preference Plurality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06674)
*Julia Sepúlveda Coelho,Scott A. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过分析75个国家1500条开放式回答，揭示人们对AI系统的期望存在根本性分歧，当前RLHF对齐方法将多元、矛盾、情境化的偏好压缩为单一奖励模型，构成系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLHF方法存在已知缺陷：聚合冲突偏好、依赖非代表性样本、仅使用二元比较。论文旨在通过实证分析人们实际想从AI中获得什么，揭示现有对齐方法的具体失败之处，指出将多元偏好扁平化为统一偏好模型的根本问题。

Method: 基于PRISM数据集，分析来自75个国家的1500条开放式回答（非二元比较），考察人们对AI系统的实际期望。通过对回答内容进行质性分析，识别人们要求的价值观、能力特征及其背后的语义分歧和情境性区分。

Result: 1) 偏好高度分散：大多数价值观仅不到25%的受访者提及，唯一例外是"真实性"（49%）；2) 同一词汇隐含不同含义：对"真实性"的理解基于不同认识论基础（有人要求有来源的声明、有人要求专家意见、有人要求非主流观点），彼此可能不兼容；3) 某些特性（如AI的类人行为、安全护栏）存在明确争议，部分人支持、部分人反对；4) 人们频繁使用情境性区分（"默认行为"vs"被请求时的行为"），这种细微差异无法被二元比较捕捉。

Conclusion: 当前对齐实践存在根本性问题：人们的偏好是情境化的、有争议的、不完美的信号，将其压缩为统一奖励模型的做法构成了'认识论暴力'。尽管用户明确要求准确性，大模型幻觉率居高不下，说明现有方法未能有效识别真实偏好。对齐方法需要从根本上反思如何处理偏好的多元性和矛盾性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are often fine-tuned through Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) to align with people's preferences and values. However, this method has known limitations: it aggregates conflicting preferences, often relies on unrepresentative samples, and uses only binary comparisons. Analysing 1,500 open-ended responses from the PRISM dataset across 75 countries, we examine what people actually want from AI systems and reveal concrete failures of current methods.
  We find that different people want different things: most values are requested by fewer than a quarter of respondents, with truthfulness the sole exception at 49%. Furthermore, the same words hide divergent meanings: when people describe what they mean by "truthfulness", they reveal distinct, potentially incompatible, epistemological bases, as some ask for sourced claims, some for expert opinions, and some even ask for unpopular views. Certain capabilities, namely how human-like a model behaves, and some features, like AI guardrails, are outright controversial, with some desiring them and others rejecting them. We additionally find that people often use contextual distinctions (what AI should do "by default" versus "if requested") that binary comparisons cannot capture.
  These findings expose fundamental problems in current alignment practices. When 49% request truthfulness but define it differently, this is unlikely to be captured by a single reward model. The persistence of high hallucination rates in well-funded models, despite users' clear demands for accuracy, suggests that current methods fail to identify actual preferences. This paper sheds light on the situated, contested, imperfect signals that are currently being flattened into universal preference models, a practice others have characterised as epistemic violence.

</details>


### [98] [HKJudge: A Legal Discourse-Annotated Corpus for Interpreting What Courts Find, How They Reason, and What They Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06679)
*Xi Xuan,Wenxin Zhang,Yufei Zhou,King-kui Sin,Chunyu Kit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个句子级专家标注的香港法院判决书话语语料库HKJudge，包含约29万句、650万词元，设计了两层话语标注方案（26种修辞角色和3种量刑要素），并在修辞角色分类和法律要素提取两项任务上对多种模型进行了基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法院判决书是法律实践和法理学的核心文本，但香港判决书的话语分析因缺乏专家标注语料库而长期受到忽视，亟需构建高质量的标注数据集来支持该领域的计算分析和法律判决预测研究。

Method: 设计了一种两层话语标注方案：句子层面为每句分配26种修辞角色之一（捕捉法院认定的事实、推理方式和裁决内容）；跨度层面标注三种量刑要素（罪名、监禁期限、罚款）。语料覆盖香港五级法院的刑事判决书，由10位法律语言学专家完成标注（标注者一致性κ=0.8）。在此基础上定义了修辞角色分类和法律要素提取两项任务，评测了四种BERT类模型、两种开源大语言模型（零样本和微调设置）以及四种商业大语言模型。

Result: 构建了包含约29万句、约650万词元的HKJudge数据集，标注者间一致性达到κ=0.8，表明标注质量较高。论文在两项任务上提供了首个基准评测结果，涵盖多种模型架构和设置的对比实验。

Conclusion: 句子级话语标注对建模香港判决书结构具有重要价值，HKJudge数据集为法律判决预测等下游任务提供了丰富的数据基础，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Court judgments are central to legal practice and jurisprudence, yet discourse analysis of Hong Kong judgments has received limited attention, owing largely to the absence of expert-annotated corpora. We introduce the Hong Kong Judgment Discourse Dataset (HKJudge), the first sentence-level expert-annotated legal discourse corpus. HKJudge includes criminal judgments across all five levels of HK's court hierarchy, comprising $\sim$290k sentences and $\sim$6.5 million tokens, fully annotated by legal linguistics experts. We design a two-tier discourse schema that captures what facts a court finds, how it reasons, and what it rules. At the sentence level, each sentence is assigned one of 26 rhetorical roles. At the span level, sentences are further annotated with three sentencing elements (charge, imprisonment term, fine). Ten legal linguistics annotators produced the annotations with an inter-annotator agreement of $κ= 0.8$. We formulate two tasks on HKJudge, termed rhetorical role classification and legal element extraction, and provide the first benchmark evaluation of four BERT-based models, two open-source LLMs under zero-shot and fine-tuning settings, and four commercial LLMs on both tasks. Our work demonstrates the value of sentence-level discourse annotation for modeling the structure of HK judgments and provides a rich data foundation for future work on legal judgment prediction. The HKJudge dataset and code are available at https://github.com/xuanxixi/HKJudge.

</details>


### [99] [Signal-Driven Observation for Long-Horizon Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06708)
*Shubham Gaur,Ian Lane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出信号驱动观察（SDO）机制，将Web智能体的观察频率与动作频率解耦，仅在特定信号触发时通过专用子调用提取任务相关DOM元素，以解决长时序任务中上下文退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Web智能体在每个动作步骤都摄入完整的DOM和无障碍树（通常数万token），导致随任务推进出现渐进式上下文退化，在任务完成前推理能力就已严重下降。作者认为将观察频率与动作频率耦合是一种架构设计错误，借鉴递归语言模型中"查询文档优于整体阅读"的洞察，主张需要重新设计观察机制。

Method: 提出信号驱动观察（Signal-Driven Observation, SDO）：设立一个专用子调用读取完整DOM，但仅返回与任务相关的元素及其选择器；该子调用不在每步触发，而是仅当轻量级信号检测器被激活时才重新调用。触发信号包括：URL跳转、新出现的可交互元素、动作执行失败、以及外部浏览器事件。

Result: 本文为立场/提案性论文（position paper），未报告实验结果，而是阐述了SDO引入的开放问题，呼吁社区将观察压缩作为Web智能体设计中的核心架构决策来研究。

Conclusion: 观察压缩应被视为Web智能体的核心架构设计问题，而非附属优化。SDO通过解耦观察与动作频率、仅在信号驱动下提取任务相关信息，为解决长时序Web任务中的上下文退化提供了新的架构范式方向。

Abstract: Web agents operating over long horizons ingest raw DOM and accessibility trees -- routinely tens of thousands of tokens -- at every action step, causing progressive context degradation that erodes reasoning well before tasks complete. We argue that this coupling of observation frequency to action frequency is an architectural mistake. Drawing on the insight from Recursive Language Models that querying a document outperforms reading it wholesale, we propose Signal-Driven Observation (SDO): a dedicated sub-call reads the full DOM but returns only task-relevant elements and their selectors, and is re-invoked only when a lightweight signal detector fires -- triggered by URL transitions, newly visible interactive elements, action failures, or exogenous browser events. We outline the open problems SDO introduces and call on the community to treat observation compression as a core architectural decision in web agent design.

</details>


### [100] [Data-Efficient Autoregressive-to-Diffusion Language Models via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06712)
*Xingyu Su,Jacob Helwig,Shubham Parashar,Atharv Chagi,Lakshmi Jotsna,Degui Zhi,James Caverlee,Dileep Kalathil,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OPDLM方法，通过在策略蒸馏（On-Policy Distillation）将自回归语言模型（ARLM）高效转化为扩散语言模型（DLM），解决了目标函数切换导致的知识丢失和训练-推理不匹配两大问题，大幅降低训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将ARLM转化为DLM的现有方法存在两个分布偏移问题：（1）从下一token预测目标切换为DLM目标会丢失ARLM预训练中获得的知识；（2）标准DLM存在训练-推理不匹配，即训练时损失定义在随机掩码序列上，而推理时使用基于置信度的解码生成轨迹，两者分布不一致。这两个问题严重制约了ARLM到DLM转化的效果和效率。

Method: 提出On-Policy Diffusion Language Model（OPDLM），核心是自蒸馏式的在策略蒸馏（self-OPD）：学生模型是将ARLM的因果注意力替换为双向注意力的模型，由学生模型自身生成解码轨迹（on-policy），教师模型为冻结的原始ARLM，在学生生成的轨迹上提供目标logits进行知识蒸馏。这样既通过在策略训练消除了DLM的训练-推理不匹配问题，又通过从原始ARLM蒸馏增强了知识保留。

Result: OPDLM在多种任务上取得了强劲的性能表现，同时仅需原有DLM预训练所需训练token数的1/15到1/7000，大幅降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: OPDLM有效解决了ARLM到DLM转化中的两个核心分布偏移问题，将DLM的转化从昂贵的预训练范式重新定位为ARLM的一种高效后训练方式，使得扩散语言模型的实用化成为可能。

Abstract: We study the transformation of autoregressive models (ARLMs) into diffusion language models (DLMs). Rather than pretraining from scratch, prior work replaces the causal attention in ARLMs with bidirectional attention and then trains the resulting model using a DLM objective. However, these approaches incur two distribution shifts. First, transitioning from a next-token prediction objective to a DLM objective can discard knowledge acquired by the ARLM during training. Second, standard DLMs suffer from a train-inference mismatch, as the training loss is defined on randomly masked sequences rather than the trajectories encountered at inference produced by confidence-based decoding. To address both challenges, we introduce an On-Policy Diffusion Language Model (OPDLM) in which On-Policy Distillation (OPD) is employed for ARLM-to-DLM transformation. Specifically, OPDLM is trained via self-OPD, where the student, an ARLM with bidirectional attention, generates its own trajectories, and the teacher, the original frozen ARLM, distills its knowledge by providing target logits on these trajectories. By training directly in an on-policy manner, OPDLM eliminates the train-inference mismatch in DLMs, while distillation from the original model enhances knowledge retention from the ARLM. Empirical results demonstrate that OPDLM requires 15x to 7,000x fewer training tokens with strong performance across a wide variety of tasks. OPDLM avoids the prohibitive cost of DLM pretraining and positions DLM transformation as a form of ARLM post-training.

</details>


### [101] [Does Topic Sentiment Cause Perceived Ideology? Comparing Human and LLM Annotations in Political News Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06715)
*Upasana Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现微调后的GPT-4o-mini在意识形态分类中学习了虚假的情感-意识形态耦合（捷径学习），该效应在人类标注中不存在，且传统F1评估无法检测此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究话题情感是否对感知的政治意识形态有因果效应，以及这一效应是否取决于标注者的身份（人类专家vs.大语言模型）。核心关切在于LLM标注作为银标签和人类判断代理在下游因果分析中的可靠性问题。

Method: 使用AllSides网站的文章，由Llama-3.3-70b-versatile提供共享情感标注，比较四种意识形态标注范式（人类专家、GPT-4o-mini基线版、GPT-4o-mini微调版、Llama-3.3-70B）。采用双重机器学习（DML）估计因果效应，并在社区层面进行中介分析，检验自然直接效应（NDE）。

Result: 人类标注在社区层面未产生显著因果效应；微调GPT-4o-mini达到最高分类准确率（F1=72.48），且是唯一在社区层面产生显著处理效应和显著自然直接效应的标注范式。这表明微调使模型内化了人类判断中不存在的虚假情感-意识形态耦合关系。

Conclusion: 微调LLM在意识形态分类任务中表现出捷径学习现象，将情感与意识形态虚假关联，而这种耦合在基于F1的评估中结构性不可见。这对使用LLM标注作为银标签以及在下游因果分析中替代人类判断具有重要警示意义——高分类性能不等于因果有效性。

Abstract: We ask whether topic sentiment has a causal effect on perceived political ideology, and whether the answer depends on who assigns the ideology label. Using articles from AllSides, paired with shared sentiment annotations from Llama-3.3-70b-versatile, we compare ideology labels from expert human annotators, GPT-4o-mini (baseline and finetuned), and Llama-3.3-70B. We apply Double Machine Learning (DML) and community-level mediation analysis across all four annotation paradigms. Human annotations yield no significant causal effects at the community level. Fine-tuned GPT-4o-mini achieves the highest classification accuracy (F1=72.48) and is the only annotator paradigm that produces significant community-level treatment effects and significant natural direct effects (NDEs) in mediation. We interpret this as evidence of shortcut learning: fine-tuning on ideology-labeled data causes the model to internalise a spurious sentiment--ideology coupling not operative in human judgment for this task. This coupling is structurally invisible to F1-based evaluation, with implications for the use of LLM annotations as silver labels and as proxies for human judgment in downstream causal analyses.

</details>


### [102] [Modular Monolingual Adaptation using Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06738)
*Nalin Kumar,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化方法，用于将预训练语言模型适配到低资源语言：替换词表、冻结对应嵌入层，仅微调模型其余部分，在苏格兰盖尔语、爱尔兰语和克丘亚语的NLU任务上表现优于全模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为低资源语言构建单语语言模型通常依赖对预训练模型进行全参数微调，但这种方式可能并非必要。作者假设全模型微调在低资源场景下存在冗余，希望通过更模块化的策略提升适配效率和性能，同时结合已有研究中语言特定分词器的优势，探索更优的适配方案。

Method: 提出一种模块化适配方法：（1）将预训练模型的词表替换为目标语言特定的分词器和词表；（2）冻结替换后的嵌入层参数；（3）仅微调模型的其余参数（即Transformer层）。在苏格兰盖尔语、爱尔兰语和克丘亚语（极低资源，仅8.5k训练样本）上进行实验，并对训练策略、预训练嵌入的选择和不同模型进行了全面分析。

Result: 在掩码填充、命名实体识别（NER）和词性标注（POS）等自然语言理解任务上的评估表明，所提出的模块化方法在将模型适配到低资源语言时优于传统的全模型微调方法，尤其在极低资源场景（如克丘亚语）下也表现出有效性。
